## Sheet: Read Me
|  | EGS Outreach Tracker v9 |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
|  | Tool-grouped · auto-fill · fixed CF · no merge cells · default font |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | When you duplicate this sheet: |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | 1. | Companies → cell B2 (yellow): type the service pitch e.g. Exhibition Stands. Service Fit auto-fills from this for every row. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | 2. | Run the Claude Code scraper from your Notion checklist. It outputs 7 tab-separated columns. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | 3. | Companies → click B7 → Paste Special → Values Only. Fills B through H. Priority (I) and Service Fit (J) auto-fill. Nothing else is touched. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | 4. | Upgrade the 10–15 obvious Tier A companies by changing their Priority dropdown. All others stay Tier B. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | 5. | Fix any wrong Industry values using the dropdown in column H. Must be from the dropdown list only. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | 6. | POCs sheet — one row per person. Fill tool sections as you enrich. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | Email for Outreach / Phone for Outreach (Aggregate block) |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | What these are: | Auto-calculated columns that pick the first available email or phone across all tool columns. Priority order: Apollo → Hunter → Lusha → Other. Use these columns when merging your outreach emails — you never need to check each tool column manually. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | All Roles — collated (Companies col P) |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | What it is: | All 5 role columns (Primary, Backup, Procurement, Gatekeeper, External Approver) joined into one cell with each role on its own line. Useful for bulk-pasting target roles into scraping tools that accept multi-line role lists. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | Conditional formatting — colour key |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | Red | Duplicate — same company name, email, or name in two rows. Merge and delete the extra. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | Amber | Missing required info (website, company, salutation, email) OR stalled outreach (4+ touches no reply, or 18+ days no reply). |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | Orange | Email bounced. Update the Outcome column. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | Blue | Tier A company — high priority target. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | Yellow | Missing optional info — all email columns empty, or Industry not classified. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | Green | Response received on any channel. Warm lead — move to sales pipeline. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
